# Skill condition audit

Client cast conditions from **SkillCond.dat** (`SkillCondTableItem`) and **Skill.dat** (`SkillTableItem`),
plus effect-row proc conditions (`SkillEffectTableConditionData` / `ESkillEffectConditionType`) documented separately below.

Generated by `python tools/skill_condition_audit.py`. `override` marks improvised rows when exports are missing.

## Condition kinds (usmap)

### ESkillCondType (SkillCond.dat CondType)

- `HasAbnormalGroupId`
- `HasAbnormalEffectType`

### ECondSelectTarget (SkillCond.dat CondSelectTarget)

- `Self`
- `Target`

### Skill.dat cast fields (grey / refuse before accept)

- `CanUseWhenElementalSummonSpawned` — Skill.dat array EElementalSummonGroupType — server **yes** (GameSession.Summons.SpiritConditionMet)
- `CanUseWhenPeaceState` — Skill.dat bool: usable only while not in combat — server **yes** (GameSession.SkillConditions.PeaceConditionMet (inCombat))
- `CanUseSkillCostType` — Skill.dat ECanUseSkillCostType + CanUseSkillCostValue — server **partial** (SkillResourceThreshold (AP types permissive: no AP on Resources yet))
- `NeedMainWeaponTypes` — Skill.dat weapon types (also enforced at cast) — server **yes** (GameSession cast: NeedMainWeaponTypes)
- `ChainSkillPrevSkillId` — Skill.dat combo chain parent (GameSession.SkillChains) — server **yes** (GameSession.SkillChains)
- `ToggleOnAbnormalId` — Skill.dat self toggle abnormal (skill_cond_grant) — server **yes** (ApplySkillCondGrant on prerequisite cast)

### SkillCondTableItem columns

- `CondType`
- `CondAbnormalGroupId`
- `CondAbnormalEffectType`
- `CondSelectTarget`
- `CondSkillList`

### ECanUseSkillCostType (Skill.dat CanUseSkillCostType)

- `HP_More`
- `HP_Less`
- `HPRatio_More`
- `HPRatio_Less`
- `MP_More`
- `MP_Less`
- `MPRatio_More`
- `MPRatio_Less`
- `AP_More`
- `AP_Less`
- `APRatio_More`
- `APRatio_Less`

### ESkillEffectConditionType (SkillEffect row proc — not the same as skill grey rules)

These gate individual effect rows (back attack, HP ratio, gauge, …). The combat server applies many only partially
via `SkillEffectSupport`; they are listed here for completeness, not as hotbar SkillCond rows.

- `Target_HasAbnormalId`
- `Target_HasAbnormalGroupId`
- `Target_HasAbnormalEffectType`
- `Target_MoveType`
- `Target_HasAbnormalId_ByCaster`
- `Target_HasAbnormalGroupId_ByCaster`
- `Target_HasAbnormalEffectType_ByCaster`
- `Caster_HasAbnormalId`
- `Caster_HasAbnormalGroupId`
- `Caster_HasAbnormalEffectType`
- `Caster_MoveType`
- `AttackAngle`
- `Probability`
- `Caster_Combat`
- `Target_Combat`
- `CalcBackAttack`
- `GatherCount`
- `Caster_MaxHPRatio_Lower`
- `Caster_MaxHPRatio_Upper`
- `Caster_MaxMPRatio_Lower`
- `Caster_MaxMPRatio_Upper`
- `Target_MaxHPRatio_Lower`
- `Target_MaxHPRatio_Upper`
- `Target_MaxMPRatio_Lower`
- `Target_MaxMPRatio_Upper`
- `Distance_Lower`
- `IsMainTarget`
- `ObjectType`
- `SameProjectileGatherCount`
- `SameProjectileGatherCount_Lower`
- `SameSubzone_TeleportCaster`
- `Distance_Height_Lower`
- `CalcFrontAttack`
- `Caster_MaxAPRatio_Lower`
- `Caster_MaxAPRatio_Upper`
- `Target_MaxAPRatio_Lower`
- `Target_MaxAPRatio_Upper`
- `Caster_Gauge_Lower`

## Summary

| Metric | Count |
| --- | ---: |
| Skills with any cast condition row | 1530 |
| Total condition rows | 2816 |
| `chain_prev` rows | 857 |
| `skill_cond.HasAbnormalEffectType` rows | 106 |
| `skill_cond.HasAbnormalGroupId` rows | 88 |
| `spirit_summon` rows | 11 |
| `toggle_grant` rows | 234 |
| `weapon` rows | 1520 |

### Server enforcement (this branch)

| Kind | Rows | Enforced | Notes |
| --- | ---: | --- | --- |
| `chain_prev` | 857 | **yes** | GameSession.SkillChains |
| `skill_cond.HasAbnormalEffectType` | 106 | **yes** | GameSession.SkillConditions + NpcTargetHasAbnormalEffect / player buffs |
| `skill_cond.HasAbnormalGroupId` | 88 | **yes** | GameSession.SkillConditions.SkillCondMet |
| `spirit_summon` | 11 | **yes** | GameSession.Summons.SpiritConditionMet |
| `toggle_grant` | 234 | **yes** | ApplySkillCondGrant on prerequisite cast |
| `weapon` | 1520 | **yes** | GameSession cast: NeedMainWeaponTypes |

## Remaining gaps

| Gap | Why |
| --- | --- |
| **SkillCond.dat export** not in repo | Committed `class-skills.json` uses `tools/data/skill-cond-overrides.json`; run `tools/skill_cond.py` with `research/local/skillcond-*/table-000.json` for full coverage. |
| **AP resource thresholds** | `ECanUseSkillCostType` AP_* values are accepted permissively until player AP exists on `Resources`. |
| **ESkillEffectConditionType** on effect rows | Proc conditions (back attack, distance, gauge equal, …) are not cast grey rules; partial support lives under combat effect application. |
| **Player-target SkillCond** | `HasAbnormalEffectType` on enemy players in PvP uses player buffs; stagger gauge is NPC-only today. |
